Fast convolution via FFT:
```
def fast_full_convolution(a):
    n=len(a)
    need=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L<need: L<<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:need]
    # remove tiny negative
    return b
```

def perturb_sequence(base, rng, mode):
    a=base.copy(); n=a.size
    # multiplicative random block
    num_blocks = rng.integers(1,4)
    for _ in range(num_blocks):
       width = int(rng.integers(max(2,n//1000), max(3,n//20)))
       start = int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
       a[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, rng.uniform(0.05,0.4)))
       if rng.random()<0.2: a[:start]? no

Validation exact:
```
def exact_score_seq(a):
    n=a.size
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <=0: return np.inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    if b.size==0: return inf
    mb=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*mb/(S*S))
```

def make_perturbed(base, rng):
    n=base.size
    a=base.copy()
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,3))
    if mode==0:
       nb=int(rng.integers(1,4))
       for _ in range(nb):
          w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//500), max(3,n//8)))
          s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
          a[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6))))
    elif mode==1:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))
       other = best? Need access top. Pass other array.
       a = (1-t)*a + t*other
    else:
       # smooth Gaussian bump scaling
       c=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       w=float(rng.uniform(n/50,n/5))
       bump=np.exp(-0.5*((np.arange(n)-c)/w)**2) * float(rng.uniform(-0.3,0.5))
       a *= np.exp(bump)
```

import numpy as np, time
try: from scipy.signal import fftconvolve? not needed

def _clean_array(x):
   try:
      a=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
   except: return None
   if a.ndim!=1 or a.size==0: return None
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)? But evaluator rejects inf/NaN; we can clip after finite check.
   a=a[np.isfinite(a)]? That changes length; better replace nonfinite with0.
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a,0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   return a

def _normalize(a):
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
   a=a/s
   # If any extremely small negative after? clip
   return a
```
Cache FFT length:
```
_FFT_CACHE={}
def _fft_len(n):
   L=_FFT_CACHE.get(n)
   if L is None:
      need=2*n-1; L=1
      while L<need: L<<=1
      _FFT_CACHE[n]=L
   return L
```
`fast_score(a)`:
```
def fast_score(a):
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
   L=_fft_len(n)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)
   nb=2*n-1
   if b.size>=nb: b=b[:nb]
   else: b=b[np.newaxis]? no.
   max_b=float(b.max())
   if not np.isfinite(max_b): return np.inf
   if max_b < 0.0: max_b=0.0
   return float(2.0*n*max_b/(s*s))
```
Potential improvement: Use `np.maximum(b,0,out=b)`? Not necessary.

`direct_score(a)` similar with np.convolve, but maybe only for exact.

`make_beta_sequence(n, alpha, beta)`:
```
def make_beta_sequence(n, alpha, beta, gamma=0.0, seed? no):
    if n<=0: return None
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    left=i+0.5
    right=n-i-0.5
    # guard positive
    logv=0.0
    if alpha !=0: logv -= alpha*np.log(left)
    if beta !=0: logv += beta*np.log(right)
    if gamma !=0: logv += gamma*(i/(n-1)-if n=1?) handle n=1.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logv)): return None
    logv -= np.max(logv)
    v=np.exp(logv)
    s=v.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    v/=s
    return v
```
For n=1, denominator n-1 zero; skip gamma if n==1. n>=1024.
Alpha,beta floats; if alpha very small, no issue.

`make_two_level(n, frac, delta)`:
```
m=int(n*frac)
a=np.empty(n); a[:m]=(1+delta)/n; a[m:]=(1-delta)/n; return normalize.
```

def local_search(base, top, deadline, rng):
   n=base.size
   best=base.copy(); best_score=fast_score(best)
   top.append...
   arange=np.arange(n)
   iters=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       mode = int(rng.integers(0,3))
       if mode==0: candidate=perturb_blocks(base? best?, rng, arange)
       elif mode==1: other=top random array; cand=(1-t)*best + t*other
       else: cand=perturb_bump(best,rng,arange,n)
       sc=fast_score(cand)
       if sc < best_score:
           best=cand; best_score=sc
           add_to_top(top,best,best_score)
       iters+=1
       if iters%1000==0 print
    return best,best_score
```

Perturb functions top-level:
```
def perturb_blocks(parent, rng, arange, min_strength=0.05, max_strength=0.6):
   a=parent.copy(); n=a.size
   nb=int(rng.integers(1,4))
   for _ in range(nb):
      wmin=max(2,n//1000); wmax=max(wmin+1,n//8)
      w=int(rng.integers(wmin,wmax))
      s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      a[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, float(rng.uniform(min_strength,max_strength))))
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   return _normalize(a)
```
Normalize returns sum1; if norm changes relative ratios? Yes.
Block multiply may only alter local; okay.

Bump:
```
def perturb_bump(parent, rng, arange):
   a=parent.copy(); n=parent.size
   c=float(rng.uniform(0,n-1))
   w=float(rng.uniform(max(3,n/200), max(4,n/10)))
   sig=float(rng.uniform(-0.3,0.8))
   a *= np.exp(sig*np.exp(-0.5*((arange-c)/w)**2))
   return _normalize(a)
```
This can create wavy.

def mix_candidates(a,b,t):

`local_search` implementation in detail:
```
def local_search(top, deadline, rng, seed):
    if not top: return None,inf
    parent = min(top)[2]
    n=parent.size
    arange=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    best=parent.copy()
    best_score=fast_score(best)
    # add best
    add_to_top(top,best,best_score)
    stop_time = deadline - 250.0
    if best_score < 1.05: stop_time = min(stop_time, time.monotonic()+120)
    elif best_score < 1.25: stop_time = min(stop_time, time.monotonic()+300)
    else: stop_time = min(stop_time, time.monotonic()+1200)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       # choose parent
       r=rng.random()
       if r < 0.85 or len(top)==1:
           p=best
       else:
           idx=int(rng.integers(0,min(8,len(top))))
           p=top[idx][2]
           if p.size != n: continue
       mode=int(rng.integers(0,4))
       if mode==0:
           cand=perturb_blocks(p,rng)
       elif mode==1:
           # random bump
           cand=perturb_bump(p,rng,arange)
       elif mode==2:
           # mix with another
           if len(top)>1:
              other_idx=int(rng.integers(0,min(10,len(top))))
              other=top[other_idx][2]
              if other.size==n:
                 t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
                 cand=_normalize(t*other+(1-t)*p)
              else: cand=None
           else: cand=perturb_blocks(p,rng)
       else:
           # random block scale plus flatten? maybe add random small noise
           cand = p.copy()
           nb=int(rng.integers(1,3))
           for _ in range(nb): ...
           cand=_normalize(cand)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=fast_score(cand)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=cand; best_score=sc; add_to_top(top,best,best_score)
       it+=1
       if it % 2000 ==0: print(...)
    return best,best_score
```

def validate_top(top, deadline):

   top_sorted = sorted(top) # no lambda

Let's implement `perturb_blocks` without using arange; `perturb_bump` uses arange.

```
def perturb_blocks(parent, rng):
    a=parent.copy(); n=a.size
    nb=1+int(rng.integers(0,3))
    for _ in range(nb):
       wmin=max(2,n//2000); wmax=max(wmin+2,n//10)
       if wmax <= wmin: wmax=wmin+1
       w=int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax+1))
       s=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
       a[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))))
    return _normalize(a)
```
`rng.integers(0,3)` returns 0-2 plus1 =>1-3.

Bump:

def perturb_bump(parent, rng, arange):
   a=parent.copy(); n=parent.size
   c=float(rng.uniform(0,n-1))
   w=float(rng.uniform(max(3,n/300), max(4,n/10)))
   sig=float(rng.uniform(-0.4,0.6))
   if sig !=0:
       z=(arange-c)/w
       a *= np.exp(sig*np.exp(-0.5*z*z))
   return _normalize(a)
```

def perturb_noise(parent,rng):

   a += rng.normal(0,1,size=a.size)*eps

```python
import numpy as np
import time


"""
Searches for a low-score nonnegative step sequence.

Main ingredients:
- A strong parametric family:
    a_i ∝ (i+1/2)^(-alpha) * (n-i-1/2)^(beta)
  normalized to sum 1.  In particular alpha ≈ beta ≈ 0.35-0.40 is very
  effective for the given score.
- Fast FFT-based scoring for thousands of candidates.
- Random smooth/block perturbations for local improvement.
- Exact direct convolution validation of the best few candidates before
  returning.
"""

_TOP_ID = 0
_FFT_CACHE = {}


def _top_id_incr():
    global _TOP_ID
    _TOP_ID += 1
    return _TOP_ID


def _clean_array(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = a.ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _normalize(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if np.any(a < 0.0):
        a[np.asanyarray(a) < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    a = a / s
    return a


def _fft_len(n):
    v = _FFT_CACHE.get(n)
    if v is None:
        need = 2 * n - 1
        L = 1
        while L < need:
            L <<= 1
        _FFT_CACHE[n] = L
        v = L
    return v


def fast_score(a):
    """Scale-invariant score using FFT convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    L = _fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if b.size > need:
        b = b[:need]
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return np.inf
    if mb < 0.0:
        mb = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))


def direct_score(a):
    """Score by direct convolution, used only for final verification."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return np.inf
    if mb < 0.0:
        mb = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))


def add_to_top(top, a, score, limit=64):
    global _TOP_ID
    if a is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if len(top) >= limit:
        top.sort()
        worst = float(top[-1][0])
        if score >= worst:
            return
        top.pop()
    top.append((float(score), _top_id_incr(), a.copy()))


def best_of_top(top):
    if not top:
        return None
    top.sort()
    return top[0]


def make_beta_sequence(n, alpha, beta, gamma=0.0):
    """
    a_i ∝ (i+1/2)^(-alpha) * (n-i-1/2)^(beta) * exp(gamma * (x-1/2))
    normalized to sum 1.
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if not (np.isfinite(alpha) and np.isfinite(beta) and np.isfinite(gamma)):
        return None
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = i + 0.5
    right = n - i - 0.5
    if alpha == 0.0 and beta == 0.0 and gamma == 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return a

    logv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if alpha != 0.0:
        logv -= alpha * np.log(left)
    if beta != 0.0:
        logv += beta * np.log(right)
    if gamma != 0.0 and n > 1:
        x = i / float(n - 1)
        logv += gamma * (x - 0.5)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logv)):
        return None
    logv -= float(np.max(logv))
    v = np.exp(logv)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return v / s


def make_beta_from_logs(n, logleft, logright, alpha, beta):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if not (np.isfinite(alpha) and np.isfinite(beta)):
        return None
    if alpha == 0.0 and beta == 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    logv = -alpha * logleft + beta * logright
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logv)):
        return None
    logv -= float(np.max(logv))
    v = np.exp(logv)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return v / s


def make_two_level(n, frac=0.5, delta=1.0 / 3.0):
    if n <= 1:
        return None
    m = int(round(n * frac))
    m = max(1, min(n - 1, m))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = (1.0 + delta) / float(n)
    a[m:] = (1.0 - delta) / float(n)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def make_random_smooth(n, rng, strength=0.9):
    m = n // 2 + 1
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    amp = 1.0 / (1.0 + 12.0 * np.abs(freq))
    zhat = (rng.normal(size=m) + 1j * rng.normal(size=m)) * amp
    z = np.fft.irfft(zhat, n)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z *= strength
    a = np.exp(z)
    return _normalize(a)


def perturb_blocks(parent, rng):
    a = parent.copy()
    n = a.size
    nb = 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 3))
    for _ in range(nb):
        wmin = max(2, n // 2000)
        wmax = max(wmin + 2, n // 10)
        w = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax + 1))
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        a[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))))
    return _normalize(a)


def perturb_bump(parent, rng, arange):
    a = parent.copy()
    n = parent.size
    c = float(rng.uniform(0.0, max(1.0, n - 1.0)))
    w = float(rng.uniform(max(3.0, n / 300.0), max(4.0, n / 10.0)))
    sig = float(rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.65))
    if sig != 0.0:
        z = (arange - c) / w
        a *= np.exp(sig * np.exp(-0.5 * z * z))
    return _normalize(a)


def perturb_noise(parent, rng):
    a = parent.copy()
    eps = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.10))
    a += eps * parent * rng.normal(size=a.size)
    return _normalize(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0))


def mix_candidates(a, b, t):
    if a is None or b is None or a.size != b.size:
        return None
    c = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
    return _normalize(np.clip(c, 0.0, 1000.0))


def local_search(top, deadline, rng):
    entry = best_of_top(top)
    if entry is None:
        return None, np.inf

    best = entry[2].copy()
    best_score = float(entry[0])
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = fast_score(best)

    n = best.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None, np.inf
    arange = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if best_score < 1.02:
        local_stop = min(deadline - 250.0, now + 120.0)
    elif best_score < 1.15:
        local_stop = min(deadline - 250.0, now + 360.0)
    elif best_score < 1.35:
        local_stop = min(deadline - 250.0, now + 900.0)
    else:
        local_stop = max(now + 60.0, deadline - 300.0)

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < local_stop:
        # Choose a parent: mostly the current best, sometimes another good one.
        if len(top) > 5 and rng.random() < 0.20:
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, 8))
            idx = min(idx, len(top) - 1)
            parent = top[idx][2]
            if parent.size != n:
                continue
        else:
            parent = best

        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
        cand = None
        if mode == 0:
            cand = perturb_blocks(parent, rng)
        elif mode == 1:
            cand = perturb_bump(parent, rng, arange)
        elif mode == 2:
            if len(top) > 1:
                j = int(rng.integers(0, 12))
                j = min(j, len(top) - 1)
                other = top[j][2]
                cand = mix_candidates(parent, other, float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30)))
            else:
                cand = perturb_blocks(parent, rng)
        else:
            cand = perturb_noise(parent, rng)

        if cand is None:
            continue

        sc = fast_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            add_to_top(top, best, best_score)

        it += 1
        if it % 3000 == 0:
            print("local iter", it, "best_fast", float(best_score))
            if time.monotonic() > local_stop:
                break

    return best, float(best_score)


def validate_top(top, deadline):
    if not top:
        return None, np.inf

    top_sorted = sorted(top)
    best_a = None
    best_exact = np.inf

    count = 0
    allow_large_once = True
    for entry in top_sorted:
        a = entry[2]
        if a is None:
            continue
        if a.size > 22000 and not allow_large_once:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
            break

        sc = direct_score(a)
        count += 1
        if allow_large_once and a.size > 22000:
            allow_large_once = False

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("exact candidate score", float(best_exact), "length", a.size)

        if count >= 6 or best_exact < 1.005:
            break

    if best_a is None:
        entry = top_sorted[0]
        best_a = entry[2].copy()
        best_exact = fast_score(best_a)
        print("no exact validation, using fast best", float(best_exact), "length", best_a.size)

    # One final authoriative check with the provided evaluator, if feasible.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0 and best_a.size <= 30000:
        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
            if np.isfinite(ev) and float(ev) < best_exact:
                best_exact = float(ev)
            print("final evaluate_sequence score", float(best_exact))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a, float(best_exact)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Returns a list of nonnegative heights.  The selected interior algorithm
    searches parametric beta-shaped sequences, then improves them by random
    local perturbations, and verifies the best exact score before returning.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 40.0)
    print("propose start, budget", float(budget_s), "deadline slack", float(deadline - start))

    top = []

    # Use any previously found construction if it exists and is usable.
    prev = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if prev is not None:
        p = _clean_array(prev)
        if p is not None and 0 < p.size <= 30000:
            p = _normalize(p)
            if p is not None:
                sc = fast_score(p)
                add_to_top(top, p, sc)
                print("previous construction fast score", float(sc), "length", p.size)

    # Parametric search over several lengths and beta/beta-family exponents.
    lengths = [1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384]
    alphas = [round(0.02 * i, 6) for i in range(0, 25)]              # 0 .. 0.48
    fixed_betas = [round(0.05 * i, 6) for i in range(0, 11)]         # 0 .. 0.50

    # Ensure we stop parametric search before the hard validation stage.
    param_limit = max(time.monotonic() + 30.0, min(deadline - 1200.0, start + 420.0))

    for n in lengths:
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        logleft = np.log(i + 0.5)
        logright = np.log(n - i - 0.5)

        # Add a few two-level fallbacks.
        for delta in (0.25, 1.0 / 3.0, 0.4, 0.5):
            a = make_two_level(n, 0.5, delta)
            if a is not None:
                add_to_top(top, a, fast_score(a), limit=64)

        for alpha in alphas:
            if alpha >= 0.55:
                break
            # Fixed beta grid.
            for beta in fixed_betas:
                if beta < 0.0 or beta > 0.65:
                    continue
                if time.monotonic() > param_limit:
                    break
                a = make_beta_from_logs(n, logleft, logright, alpha, beta)
                if a is not None:
                    add_to_top(top, a, fast_score(a), limit=64)

            # Special betas near and above alpha.
            for beta in (alpha, alpha + 0.02, alpha + 0.05, 0.5 * alpha):
                if beta < 0.0 or beta > 0.65:
                    continue
                if time.monotonic() > param_limit:
                    break
                a = make_beta_from_logs(n, logleft, logright, alpha, beta)
                if a is not None:
                    add_to_top(top, a, fast_score(a), limit=64)

        if time.monotonic() > param_limit:
            break

        # A few randomized smooth starts for this length.
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > param_limit:
                break
            a = make_random_smooth(n, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.1)))
            if a is not None:
                add_to_top(top, a, fast_score(a), limit=64)

    # If the quick param pass is weak, do a denser pass before local search.
    best_entry = best_of_top(top)
    best_score = best_entry[0] if best_entry is not None else np.inf
    if best_entry is None or np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 1.45:
        print("denser param pass triggered, current best", float(best_score))
        denser_end = max(time.monotonic() + 30.0, min(deadline - 800.0, start + 1200.0))
        for n in [4096, 8192, 16384]:
            i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
            logleft = np.log(i + 0.5)
            logright = np.log(n - i - 0.5)
            for ai in range(8, 26):
                alpha = 0.02 * ai
                for bi in range(0, 14):
                    beta = 0.04 * bi
                    if time.monotonic() > denser_end:
                        break
                    for b2 in (beta, alpha, beta + 0.02):
                        if b2 < 0.0 or b2 > 0.65:
                            continue
                        a = make_beta_from_logs(n, logleft, logright, alpha, b2)
                        if a is not None:
                            add_to_top(top, a, fast_score(a), limit=64)
            if time.monotonic() > denser_end:
                break

    best_entry = best_of_top(top)
    if best_entry is None:
        # Very safe fallback: constant sequence has score 2.
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        print("fallback uniform")
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    print("param best fast score", float(best_entry[0]), "length", best_entry[2].size)

    # Local improvement.
    local_best, local_score = local_search(top, deadline, rng)
    if local_best is not None and np.isfinite(local_score):
        add_to_top(top, local_best, local_score, limit=64)
        print("local best fast score", float(local_score))

    # Exact verification and final selection.
    final_a, final_exact = validate_top(top, deadline)
    if final_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(final_a)):
        final_a = best_entry[2]

    final_a = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64)
    final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_a.size == 0:
        final_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
    s = float(np.sum(final_a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        final_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
    else:
        final_a = final_a / s

    try:
        final_exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_a]))
    except Exception:
        final_exact = float("inf")

    print("FINAL returned length", final_a.size, "final score", final_exact)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
        print("returning at deadline margin")

    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
